Rust crates (from crates.io)
============================

Version:  Cargo.lock's
From:     https://crates.io/
Used by:  !PDF (rospdf: hayro, the PDF interpreter, and vello_cpu, the
          rasteriser), pdfsvc (pdf-writer), and the ROSAPPS engines
          (!Scribe, !Trace, !Palette, !Ledger, !Folio), which are built from
          the ArtCraft libraries and the crates those use
Licence:  MIT, Apache 2.0, BSD, zlib, Unicode, Unlicense, per crate

The crates, with the licence each declares:
